## WHY THIS PASS EXISTS (the repair chain, and what makes it terminate)

## N. PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED SO NOBODY SPENDS AN ANSWER RE-DERIVING THEM

Settled by pass 1 / pass 2 / the pass-2 builder (panel_20260903_121558 and
`regress/artifacts/pcc2widthkills/opus_pass2_20260903/RESULTS.md`), warrants inline:

1. **The width is LEGAL** -- `mvme_pcctworeg.h`: the registers "can be accessed as bytes,
   two-bytes, or four-bytes".  Tree header, not a guess.
2. **`memory_readmax64` composes BIG-ENDIAN**, so `(uint8_t) idata` is the HIGHEST-address
   lane; a bare deletion of the guard stores the NEIGHBOUR's byte.  MEASURED: `M_DELETE` ->
   `rb genctl` reads 0xbb.
3. **Reachability, CORRECTED by pass 2:** every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked
   before memory_rw and `relative_addr %= 0x40` keeps parity, so the SIX even-offset blocks
   (GENCTL 0x02, TxCTL 0x16, TxICR 0x1a, SCCERR 0x1c, SCCTX 0x1e, IPL 0x3e) are GUEST-reachable
   and the SIX odd ones (VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, SCCICR, SCCRX, SCSIICR, MASK) are OPERATOR-reachable.
   The old comment said five; IPL at 0x3e is even.
4. **The operator path reaches the SAME guard.** Debugger `put h` at an odd address warns, then
   calls `store_16bit_word` -> ONE `memory_rw(len 2)` -> the same `DEVICE_ACCESS` arm a guest
   would reach (verified by the builder from source, and MEASURED pre-fix: exit1).  Guest byte
   stores (`st.b`) at odd offsets are fine -- only non-byte accesses are alignment-checked.
5. **Arm bodies, read from the file (inlined below): SCCICR stores `idata & 0x1f`; SCCRX and
   SCSIICR store `idata & 0xdf`; each then calls `reassert_interrupts()`.  PSCALEADJ's write
   body STORES NOTHING -- it only prints "write to PSCALEADJ ignored (value ...)".**
   Seven of the twelve blocks call `reassert_interrupts()` (derived by awk over the arms; the
   earlier "six" was wrong and is corrected in the comment).
6. **`fatal()` returns** (`debugmsg.c`: no early-out, no exit).  The decline announces once per
   block per instance via `d->warned`, a 12-bit field written ONLY by `pcc2_width_decline()`.
7. **NOT fixed and cited:** `pcc2c040kill` (a legal byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends the
   host in `reassert_interrupts`), `pcc2defaultkill`, `pcc2counter8`.  Every GENCTL lane value
   in the probe keeps bit 0x04 clear.
8. **The pass-2 ruling, adopted:** clause (d) is a FLOOR.  A guard kept with its decline nulled
   while the write lands (announce-then-land) at the four odd arms was "a sentence owed" in the
   repair's own PRETABLE Section 2; the builder asked whether a disclosure can discharge a floor
   clause.  It cannot.  Four rows were added instead (Section 5 of PRETABLE, inlined below).
9. **Pass-2 roster:** eight of nine answered (agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED, reserved for
   measurement); the Opus seat was the sole builder and then ran its own pass 3 unprompted.

## QUESTIONS

1. **THE PREMISE AUDIT, as questions.**  (a) Is the clause-(d) enumeration now CLOSED?  The
   inputs are `len`, `writeflag`, `relative_addr` (-> block), `idata`, `d->warned`.  The shapes
   measured are: guard deleted, `len != 1` -> `len > 2`, verbatim `exit(1)` reinstated, latch-bit
   ALIAS (one enum member for two blocks), announce-then-fall-through, and the operand swap
   `len != 1` -> `idata > 0xff`.  Enumerate 12 blocks x 6 shapes = 72 cells and say WHICH cells
   have a measured kill, which are argued equivalent, and which are UNMEASURED.  Do not accept
   "every block has a dcl row" as coverage of the fall-through shape -- pass 2 found that a
   `dcl` row cannot vary under a mutant that keeps announcing.
   (b) Is `latch all` a sound CARDINALITY row?  It writes one wide 0x0003 per BLOCK through the
   operator path and expects TWELVE announces.  Can any benign traffic in that session set a bit
   early so that an aliased pair still reads 12?  Can two blocks announce on one bit and still
   count 12 (e.g. because the session touches a block twice)?
   (c) Is `op pscale noig` a REAL channel -- does PSCALEADJ's "ignored" print reach the pty
   transcript the row counts, and is 0 the right expectation on the fix?
   (d) Are the three new readbacks (`rb sccicr/sccrx/scsiicr`: st.b 0x1f, put h 0xaab8, ld.bu ->
   0x1f) correct on the FIX?  Under a fall-through they land 0x18 (& 0x1f) or 0x98 (& 0xdf) and
   call `reassert_interrupts()` -- could that call END THE HOST (the pcc2c040kill class) and
   make the row "died" rather than a readback?  Either is red, but the record should say which.

2. **THE SMALLEST EDIT THAT BREAKS THIS AND STILL PASSES 82 ROWS.**  Name the row you expect to
   stay green.  Shapes NOT in the tables above are the interesting ones.

   split (reads ACCEPT, writes DECLINE + latched announce) was settled in pass 1 and is not
   re-litigated.  Say so if you disagree that it is N/A.

4. **IS THE RECORD HONEST?**  The contract (probe header) and the PRETABLE sections are inlined.
   Name any sentence that a measurement above refutes, any count without a deriver, and any

## THE ENCLOSING CODE (the helper and the four odd arms whose rows are new; whole arms, both

```c
static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
{
	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
	}

	case PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN, "PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			fatal("[ pcc2: write to PSCALEADJ ignored (value "
			    "0x%02x) ]\n", (int)idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T2CTL:
	case PCCTWO_T1CTL:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxCTL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			/*  PCC2_TCTL_CEN and PCC2_TCTL_COC:  */
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= 0xfc;
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 3);

			if (idata & PCC2_TCTL_COVF)
				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &=
				    ~(PCC2_TCTL_OVF | PCC2_TCTL_COVF);

			if (idata & ~7)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxCTL"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0x1f;
			if (idata & ~0x1f)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCTX:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCTX_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCTX", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0x1f;
			if (idata & ~0x1f)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCTX"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCRX:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCRX_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCRX", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xdf;
			if (idata & ~0xdf)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCRX"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCRXIACK:
		/*  TODO. Hm. Is this enough?  */
		d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] &= ~PCC2_IRQ_INT;
		reassert_interrupts(d);
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCSIICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCSIICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCSIICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xdf;
			if (idata & ~0xdf)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCSIICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_IPL:
		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like thg.
+t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12") + program([ST_B(5, MASK)])
+                        + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaabb" % (PCC2 + MASK)]
+                        + program([LD_BU(4, MASK)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
+row("op mask", how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd MASK survives")
+row("op mask dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_MASK " + DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
+row("rb mask", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x12",
+    "MASK unchanged after the declined wide write -- GENCTL's twin gets GENCTL's readback")
+
 # ---------------------------------------------------------------------- summary
 print("NOROW  two device instances               -- mvme187 has ONE pcc2; per-instance latch state"
       " is by construction, not measured")

```

## PRETABLE.out (the pre-table block, written BEFORE table 2 part 1; Section 5 before part 2)

```
PRE-TABLE BLOCK -- pcc2widthkills pass-2 REPAIR table (killtable2_20260903), pcc2_width_probe.py
================================================================================

*** SECTION 0 -- THE HONEST PART: THIS ROUND'S TABLE 1 (killtable_20260903, 8 mutants, 60
*** rows) RAN WITH NO PRE-TABLE BLOCK.  The pass-2 measuring seat named the omission and
*** measured what the skipped SIBLING-PARITY check would have shown: GENCTL and MASK have
*** byte-identical write bodies, GENCTL carried five row mentions and MASK zero, and the
*** MASK null-repair was a 60/60 sweep.  The class -- aliasing a latch constant, one token,
*** clause (d) -- sat in sgideprimop's own PRETABLE.out 33 hours earlier, and this round
*** transferred that file's d->warned design without the check that qualified it.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
This block is written BEFORE table 2 runs; its mtime against the table-2 .out files
beside it proves the order for table 2 only.  Table 1 is recorded as HAVING HAD NO
PRE-TABLE BLOCK, not as having passed one.  (Third recorded skip of the mandate.)

SECTION 1 -- GUARD FIELD vs BODY FIELD
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GUARD reads (each of 12 blocks):  len, writeflag
DECLINE body reads:               d->warned (bit test), len (printed)     writes: d->warned |= bit
FALL-THROUGH body reads:          idata, relative_addr, d->pcctwo_reg[], pending_timer*_interrupts

Where they differ: the guard reads the ACCESS SHAPE, the body reads the VALUE.
Disagreeing states: (len==1, any idata)      -> acc rows: byte writes land
                    (len==2, idata > 0xff)   -> every hwr/rb/wwr/latch row
                    (len==2, idata <= 0xff)  -> NO ROW EXISTED.  All 77 wide-write rows used
                    values above 0xff, so a guard operand swap `len != 1' -> `idata > 0xff'
                    (a plausible "fits a byte" reading) was green by construction while a
                    big-endian st.h 0x0022 stored 0x22 -- the neighbour's lane -- into GENCTL.
  ==> ADDED before the table: `rb genctl lo' (st.b 0x11; st.h 0x0022; expect 0x11) and the
      `latch all' value changed 0x0303 -> 0x0003 so the cardinality row reds under the same
      swap at ANY arm.  Mutant M_IDATA_GENCTL built to measure it.  This is the block's own
      yield, found by asking the field question rather than the mutant question.

THE SHARED-BITFIELD QUESTION (who else writes d->warned, can they set my bit?):
    occurrences of 'warned |=' in dev_pcc2.c: 1      lines mentioning 'warned': 7
  One writer, pcc2_width_decline(), sets exactly the bit its caller names; nothing in
  DEVICE_ACCESS or a tick writes the field, so benign traffic cannot pre-set a bit.  What
  CAN collide is the ENUM: two callers naming one member.  That is the M_ALIAS_SCCTX shape,
  and `latch all' (12 announces, one per block) is the row that sees it -- `latch same' and
  `latch two' pin one pair of 66 and do not.

SECTION 2 -- SIBLING PARITY, ARM BY ARM (after the repair; every block owes a survival row,
a decline-announce row, and a readback where decline != accept)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  block      even?  survival            announce                  readback (decline != accept?)
  GENCTL     even   hwr genctl(+lv)     rb genctl dcl             rb genctl, val h genctl, rb genctl lo, acc genctl
  VECBASE    odd    op vecbase          op vecbase dcl            rb vecbase
  PSCALEADJ  odd    op pscale           op pscale dcl             plain store, no mask: dcl is the channel
  TxCTL      even   hwr t2ctl(+lv)      rb t2ctl dcl              rb t2ctl (& 3), acc t2ctl, op t1ctl(+dcl)
  TxICR      even   hwr t2icr(+lv)      rb t2icr dcl              rb t2icr (& 0x17), acc t2icr, op t1icr(+dcl)
  SCCERR     even   hrd/hwr/wrd/wwr     hwr sccerr dcl, wwr dcl   VALUE-IDENTICAL (always 0): message only, disclosed
  SCCICR     odd    op sccicr           op sccicr dcl             dcl is the channel
  SCCTX      even   hwr scctx(+lv)      rb scctx dcl              rb scctx (& 0x1f), acc scctx
  SCCRX      odd    op sccrx            op sccrx dcl              dcl is the channel
  SCSIICR    odd    op scsiicr          op scsiicr dcl            dcl is the channel
  IPL        even   hrd/hwr ipl(+lv)    hwr ipl dcl               read-side IPL==0 diagnostic (width-independent)
  MASK       odd    op mask             op mask dcl               rb mask (GENCTL's twin: plain store + reassert)
  latch all  (all twelve, one offset per BLOCK -> 12 announces)
Asymmetry left, DISCLOSED: PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR have no readback row.  Their `dcl'
rows red under any guard edit that lets the halfword land silently (len > 2, delete, operand
swap) -- every shape in this table.  A guard edit that lands the write AND still announces
(the M_FALL_MASK shape) at one of those four would escape; that is the sentence owed, and
MASK's own fall-through is the measured instance of the shape.

SECTION 3 -- CLAIM -> ROW (added comment sentences asserting an observable)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  "kills a guard edited at THIS arm"            -> rb t2ctl / rb t2icr / rb scctx   (M_LENGT2_TXCTL/TXICR/SCCTX)
  "TWELVE announces: bit CARDINALITY"           -> latch all                          (M_ALIAS_SCCTX)
  "0x3e is EVEN -- GUEST-reachable, survives"   -> hwr ipl, hwr ipl lv                (M_KILL_IPL)
  "value-identical arm, message channel"        -> hwr sccerr dcl                     (M_LENGT2_SCCERR)
  "guard is on len, not idata"                  -> rb genctl lo                       (M_IDATA_GENCTL)
  "six guest-reachable" (contract + comment)    -> hwr ipl                            (census row, not a kill)
  "seven of the twelve blocks call reassert"    -> DERIVED by awk over the arms; no row (not an observable of the fix)

SECTION 4 -- CHANNEL: mutant -> row -> observable that differs.  PREDICTIONS, written
before any table-2 cell landed.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  M_DELETE_MASK    rb mask 0xbb (was 0x0), op mask dcl 0            MASK guard deleted: halfword lands the neighbour lane
  M_KILL_IPL       hwr ipl exit1, hwr ipl lv died                  verbatim kill at a GUEST-reachable arm
  M_KILL_MASK      op mask exit1                                    kill at the operator-reachable twin
  M_DELETE (ctl)   rb genctl 0xbb, rb genctl dcl 0                  the table-1 control still reds
  M_DELETE_TXICR   rb t2icr 0x10, rb t2icr dcl 0                    0xaab8 & 0x17 lands
  M_LENGT2_TXICR   same                                             per-arm `len > 2'
  M_LENGT2_TXCTL   rb t2ctl 0x0, rb t2ctl dcl 0                     0xaab8 & 3 lands
  M_LENGT2_SCCTX   rb scctx 0x18, rb scctx dcl 0                    0xaab8 & 0x1f lands
  M_LENGT2_SCCERR  hwr sccerr dcl 0                                 value-identical: the announce is the only channel
  M_ALIAS_SCCTX    latch all 11 (SCCTX announces on SCCRX's bit)    a collision drops the count; nothing else moves
  M_LENGT2_MASK    rb mask 0xbb, op mask dcl 0                      halfword lands at MASK
  M_FALL_MASK      rb mask 0xbb (op mask dcl stays 1)               announce-then-land: readback is the only channel
  M_IDATA_GENCTL   rb genctl lo 0x22, latch all 11                  operand swap to a value test: a small halfword lands
  BASE             78/78                                            the repair perturbed nothing
Every row above names a mutant under which its channel VARIES; no row is survival-only at a
refusal-effect site.  Rows whose channel cannot vary under any enumerated mutant: none added.


SECTION 5 -- AMENDMENT, written after table-2 PART 1 (9 mutants + BASE, all killed) and
BEFORE PART 2.  Section 2's "sentence owed" was RULED A FLOOR BREACH, not a disclosure.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The measuring seat, running its own pass 3 against the 78-row detector, asked whether the
disclosed asymmetry (PSCALEADJ/SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR: no readback, so a guard that ANNOUNCES
and then lets the halfword land is invisible) can be discharged by clause (e).  Ruling: NO.
Clause (d) is a FLOOR -- "the guard kept, its effect removed" -- and the fall-through shape at
those arms is exactly that.  Clause (e) governs claims ABOVE the floor; a disclosure cannot
place a detector below it, or every floor breach becomes dischargeable by a sentence.  The
CHANNEL rule in Section 4 already said as much: a `dcl' row cannot vary under a mutant that
keeps announcing, so at those four arms it was the WRONG channel for this shape.

Rows added (part 2 measures them):
  rb sccicr / rb sccrx / rb scsiicr   set 0x1f by byte, decline put h 0xaab8, read back 0x1f
                                      M_FALL_* lands 0x18 (& 0x1f) / 0x98 (& 0xdf) and REASSERTS
  op pscale noig                      PSCALEADJ's body stores nothing and only prints; count of
                                      "PSCALEADJ ignored" in the wide-write session == 0.
                                      M_FALL_PSCALEADJ prints it once.
Blast radius is NOT uniform across the four (the seat's point): three of them put the
neighbour's lane into an INTERRUPT CONTROL register and reassert; one prints.  One sentence
had covered all four, which was too coarse as well as below the floor.

```

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

C:\DocumentNoSnc\CC\GXEMUL\GXEMUL-SEC\tools\pipeline\ledger.json:26590:     "outcome_note": "*** THE ROW'S OWN HELD QUESTION Q1 -- `IS A WIDE ACCESS EVEN REACHABLE?  If it cannot be, ten guards are unreachable and this row is DOCUMENTATION' -- IS ANSWERED: YES, AND BY THE MOST ORDINARY ACCESS THERE IS.  Settled by reading, in about two minutes, per the rule that anything checkable in two minutes gets CHECKED rather than judged. ***\n\nTHE CHAIN, each link read rather than assumed:\n\n1. `cpu_m88k_instr_loadstore.c` is generated for `LS_SIZE` of 1, 2, 4 AND 8 -- the m88k ISA has byte, halfword, word and doubleword load/store, so wide accesses exist at the CPU.\n2. Alignment is enforced, and enforced as an EXCEPTION rather than a silent mask: `if (addr & (LS_SIZE - 1)) m88k_exception(cpu, M88K_EXCEPTION_MISALIGNED_ACCESS, 0);`.  So a MISALIGNED wide access never reaches memory_rw -- but an ALIGNED one is untouched by that guard.\n3. The access is delivered AT FULL WIDTH IN ONE CALL, not split into bytes: the buffer is `uint8_t data[LS_SIZE]` and the call is `cpu->memory_rw(cpu, cpu->mem, addr, data, sizeof(data), MEM_READ, memory_rw_flags)`.  `sizeof(data)` IS `LS_SIZE`.  So a halfword load arrives at the device handler with `len == 2`.\n4. The registers are CONSECUTIVE BYTES and the boundary is EVEN: `mvme_pcctworeg.h` gives `PCCTWO_GENCTL 0x0002` and `PCCTWO_VECBASE 0x0003` (also `PCCTWO_IPL 0x003e` / `PCCTWO_MASK 0x003f`).  **0x0002 is 2-ALIGNED.**\n\n*** THEREFORE: a guest halfword load at offset 0x0002 spans GENCTL and VECBASE, takes no misalignment exception, reaches DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) with len == 2, hits `case PCCTWO_GENCTL:` and dies on `if (len != 1) { fatal(); exit(1); }`.  A SINGLE NATURALLY-ALIGNED HALFWORD LOAD ENDS THE HOST. ***\n\nThat is the same shape as `#499`'s finding -- an ordinary access, no unusual width choice needed in the sense of `unusual', because a halfword read of two adjacent byte registers is exactly what a driver does when it reads a register PAIR.  It is not an exotic probe.\n\nCONSEQUENCE FOR THE ROW: it is a LIVE DEFECT, not documentation, and its HIGH severity stands.  The disposition question (which of the six members) is unchanged and still open; what is settled is that the question is worth asking.\n\nWHAT THIS DOES NOT SETTLE, stated so the next round does not over-read it: it establishes reachability for a LOAD spanning two byte registers.  Whether a real MVME driver actually issues one, and whether the WRITE direction is equally reachable, are separate questions -- the row's own Q1 flagged the read/write asymmetry and this note does not close that half.  A rung-3 witness already exists for this row; the natural next step is to extend it with an aligned halfword access at 0x0002 and record which direction it was taken in.",

 def program(insns, at=CODE):
+    #  `at' lets a session run TWO programs: a second sequence at the SAME address finds
+    #  pc already there and the assignment is skipped (the Addr hazard, designed out in
+    #  the sgi probes with Addr.take()).  Callers needing two pass at=CODE + 0x100.
     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
     cmds = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, w) for i, w in enumerate(words)]
     return cmds + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]

+# ------------------------- latch CARDINALITY (pass 2, the measuring seat's class closer)
+#  `latch same' and `latch two' pin ONE pair out of 66: every other session writes at most
+#  one BLOCK, so a bit shared by any other pair announces normally and no row moves.
+#  MEASURED by the builder: aliasing SCCTX onto SCCRX's bit -- a ONE-CHARACTER edit --
+#  left the 60-row detector at 60/60.  ONE OFFSET PER BLOCK: TxCTL serves T1CTL+T2CTL
+#  and TxICR serves T1ICR+T2ICR, so twelve LABELS would give only ten announces -- the
+#  same units slip that let "five even blocks + seven odd ones = twelve" read as all
+#  twelve blocks.  This row pins the CARDINALITY of the latch: any collision anywhere in
+#  the 12-bit space drops the count.  (Operator path for all twelve, so the odd ones are
+#  reachable; the guest-reachable halves have their own rows above.)
+BLOCKS12 = (("GENCTL", 0x02), ("VECBASE", 0x03), ("PSCALEADJ", 0x15), ("TxCTL", 0x16),

+#  VALUE 0x0003, not 0x0303: it fits a byte, so this row ALSO reds if any arm swaps its

 255: static void reassert_interrupts(struct pcc2_data *d)
 256: {
 257: 	int assert = 0;
 258: 
 259: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_GENCTL] & PCC2_C040) {
 260: 		/*  The M68000 interrupt mechanism involves outputting
 261: 		    interrupt level on pins EIPL<2..0>. Not implemented
 262: 		    yet.  */
 263: 		fatal("pcc2: C040 interrupt assertions... TODO\n");
 264: 		exit(1);
 265: 	}
 266: 
 267: 	/*
 268: 	 *  Recalculate current interrupt level:
 269: 	 */
 270: 	d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = 0;
 271: 
 272: 	/*  Timer interrupts?  */
 273: 	if (d->pending_timer1_interrupts > 0 &&
 274: 	    d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T1ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN)
 275: 		d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T1ICR] |= PCC2_TTIRQ_INT;
 276: 	if (d->pending_timer2_interrupts > 0 &&
 277: 	    d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T2ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN)
 278: 		d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T2ICR] |= PCC2_TTIRQ_INT;
 279: 
 280: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T1ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT) {
 281: 		int intlevel = d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T1ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_IL;
 282: 		d->cur_int_vec[intlevel] = PCC2V_TIMER1;
 283: 		if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] < intlevel)
 284: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = intlevel;
 285: 	}
 286: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T2ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT) {
 287: 		int intlevel = d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_T2ICR] & PCC2_TTIRQ_IL;
 288: 		d->cur_int_vec[intlevel] = PCC2V_TIMER2;
 289: 		if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] < intlevel)
 290: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = intlevel;
 291: 	}
 292: 
 293: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCTX] & PCC2_IRQ_IEN &&
 294: 	    d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCTX] & PCC2_IRQ_INT) {
 295: 		int intlevel = d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCTX] & PCC2_IRQ_IPL;
 296: 		d->cur_int_vec[intlevel] = PCC2V_SCC_TX;
 297: 		if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] < intlevel)
 298: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = intlevel;
 299: 	}
 300: 
 301: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] & PCC2_IRQ_IEN &&
 302: 	    d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] & PCC2_IRQ_INT) {
 303: 		int intlevel = d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] & PCC2_IRQ_IPL;
 304: 		d->cur_int_vec[intlevel] = PCC2V_SCC_RX;
 305: 		if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] < intlevel)
 306: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = intlevel;
 307: 	}
 308: 
 309: 	if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCSIICR] & PCC2_IRQ_IEN &&
 310: 	    d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCSIICR] & PCC2_IRQ_INT) {
 311: 		int intlevel = d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCSIICR] & PCC2_IRQ_IPL;
 312: 		d->cur_int_vec[intlevel] = PCC2V_SCSI;
 313: 		if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] < intlevel)
 314: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] = intlevel;
 315: 	}
 316: 
 317: 	/*  TODO: Other interrupt sources.  */
 318: 
 319: 	/*  Assert interrupt on the CPU if the IPL is higher than the mask:  */
 320: 	if ((d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] & INTERRUPT_LEVEL_MASK) >
 321: 	    (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_MASK] & INTERRUPT_LEVEL_MASK))
 322: 		assert = 1;
 323: 
 324: 	debug("[ pcc2: IPL = %i, MASK = %i => %s ]\n", d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL]& INTERRUPT_LEVEL_MASK,
 325: 		d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_MASK] & INTERRUPT_LEVEL_MASK,
 326: 		assert ? "ASSERT" : "no assert");
 327: 
 328: 	/*  ... but only allow interrupts if Master Interrupt Enable is on:  */
 329: 	if (!(d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_GENCTL] & PCC2_MIEN))
 330: 		assert = 0;
 331: 
 332: 	if (assert)
 333: 		INTERRUPT_ASSERT(d->cpu_irq);
 334: 	else
 335: 		INTERRUPT_DEASSERT(d->cpu_irq);
 336: }

 338: 
 339: void pcctwo_interrupt_common(struct interrupt *interrupt, int assert)
 340: {
 341: 	struct pcc2_data *d = (struct pcc2_data *) interrupt->extra;
 342: 
 343: 	switch (interrupt->line) {
 344: 
 345: 	case PCC2V_SCSI:
 346: 		if (assert)
 347: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCSIICR] |= PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 348: 		else
 349: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCSIICR] &= ~PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 350: 		break;
 351: 
 352: 	case PCC2V_SCC_TX:
 353: 		if (assert)
 354: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCTX] |= PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 355: 		else
 356: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCTX] &= ~PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 357: 		break;
 358: 
 359: 	case PCC2V_SCC_RX:
 360: 		if (assert)
 361: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] |= PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 362: 		else
 363: 			d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] &= ~PCC2_IRQ_INT;
 364: 		break;
 365: 
 366: 	default:fatal("[ pcctwo_interrupt_common: TODO: line = %i ]\n",
 367: 		    interrupt->line);
 368: 		exit(1);
 369: 	}
 370: 
 371: 	reassert_interrupts(d);
 372: }
 373: 
 374: 
 375: static void pcctwo_interrupt_assert(struct interrupt *interrupt)
 376: {
 377: 	pcctwo_interrupt_common(interrupt, 1);
 378: }
 379: static void pcctwo_interrupt_deassert(struct interrupt *interrupt)
 380: {
 381: 	pcctwo_interrupt_common(interrupt, 0);
 382: }

 385: DEVICE_TICK(pcc2)
 386: {
 387: 	struct pcc2_data *d = (struct pcc2_data *) extra;
 388: 	reassert_interrupts(d);
 389: }

 391: 
 392: /*  pcc2widthkills: decline a wide WRITE to a byte register, announcing once per
 393:     block per instance.  The caller `break's after this; the read side of the same
 394:     block never reaches here.  */
 395: static void pcc2_width_decline(struct pcc2_data *d, int bit, const char *name, int len)
 396: {
 397: 	if (!(d->warned & (1u << bit))) {
 398: 		d->warned |= (1u << bit);
 399: 		fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented len=%i write to %s -- declined."
 400: 		    "  (once) ]\n", len, name);
 401: 	}
 402: }
 403: 
 404: DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2)
 405: {
 406: 	uint64_t idata = 0;
 407: 	struct pcc2_data *d = (struct pcc2_data *) extra;
 408: 
 409: 	/*  0xfff42000..0xfff42fff, but only 0x40 unique registers:  */
 410: 	relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE;
 411: 
 412: 	if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 413: 		idata = memory_readmax64(cpu, data, len);
 414: 
 415: 	if (writeflag == MEM_READ) {
 416: 		if (relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
 417: 			memcpy(data, d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, len);
 418: 		else {
 419: 			static int warned = 0;	/* #119: loud-once (course-correction) */
 420: 			if (!warned) {
 421: 				fatal("[ pcc2: read 0x%x+%i beyond reg space 0x%x; "
 422: 				    "zero-filled ]\n", (int)relative_addr, (int)len,
 423: 				    (int)PCC2_SIZE);
 424: 				warned = 1;
 425: 			}
 426: 			memset(data, 0, len);	/* OB-11b: don't leak uninit host memory */
 427: 		}
 428: 	}
 429: 
 430: 	switch (relative_addr) {
 431: 
 432: 	case PCCTWO_CHIPID:
 433: 	case PCCTWO_CHIPREV:
 434: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 435: 			fatal("[ IGNORING write to read-only PCCTWO_CHIPID/"
 436: 			    "CHIPREV register ]\n");
 437: 		}
 438: 		break;
 439: 
 440: 	case PCCTWO_GENCTL:
 441: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 442: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_GENCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_GENCTL", len);
 443: 			break;
 444: 		}
 445: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 446: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata;
 447: 			reassert_interrupts(d);
 448: 		}
 449: 		break;
 450: 
 451: 	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
 452: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 453: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
 454: 			break;
 455: 		}
 456: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 457: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
 458: 			if (idata & ~0xf0)
 459: 				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
 460: 				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
 461: 		}
 462: 		break;
 463: 
 464: 	case PCCTWO_T1CMP:
 465: 	case PCCTWO_T2CMP:
 466: 	case PCCTWO_T1COUNT:
 467: 	case PCCTWO_T2COUNT:
 468: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
 469: 		    relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
 470: 			memcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);
 471: 		break;
 472: 
 473: 	case PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ:
 474: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 475: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN, "PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ", len);
 476: 			break;
 477: 		}
 478: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 479: 			fatal("[ pcc2: write to PSCALEADJ ignored (value "
 480: 			    "0x%02x) ]\n", (int)idata);
 481: 		}
 482: 		break;
 483: 
 484: 	case PCCTWO_T2CTL:
 485: 	case PCCTWO_T1CTL:
 486: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 487: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxCTL", len);
 488: 			break;
 489: 		}
 490: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 491: 			/*  PCC2_TCTL_CEN and PCC2_TCTL_COC:  */

   1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2: """pcc2widthkills DETECTOR -- the twelve width-guard blocks in DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2).
   3: 
   4: WHAT THE FIX DID.  Twelve `if (len != 1) { fatal(...); exit(1); }' blocks (ten kills and
   5: two #119 function-static latches) ended the host on a guest-chosen access width.  The
   6: tree's own header says PCC2 registers "can be accessed as bytes, two-bytes, or
   7: four-bytes", so the width is LEGAL.  The fix is a DIRECTION SPLIT:
   8: 
   9:     READS   ACCEPT -- the memcpy above the switch already served them, raw bytes in
  10:             address order, width-correct by construction.  The guard discarded a
  11:             correct answer.
  12:     WRITES  DECLINE + per-instance latched announce -- every write arm uses `idata',
  13:             the big-endian-composed value, which is "the addressed byte" ONLY at
  14:             len==1.  Deleting the guard would store the NEIGHBOUR's lane.
  15: 
  16: CONTRACT (vacuity-floor clause (e)) -- what this detector is designed to catch:
  17: 
  18:   CATCHES
  19:     * reinstatement of the kill at any of the SIX GUEST-reachable blocks (survival
  20:       rows paired with a liveness observable, because exit status cannot tell
  21:       "declined and quit" from "died with status 0");
  22:     * *** pass 2: the two FOLDED-IN arms, IPL and MASK, had NO ROW -- twelve blocks
  23:       converted, ten given rows, and the shipped contract did not say so.  A seat
  24:       found it by sibling parity: MASK is GENCTL's twin and had none of GENCTL's rows.
  25:       Repaired: IPL is EVEN and therefore the SIXTH guest-reachable block (survival +
  26:       message rows); MASK is odd (operator rows) and gets GENCTL's readback. ***
  27:     * reinstatement at any of the seven OPERATOR-reachable blocks, through the
  28:       debugger's `put h' at an odd address -- MEASURED to warn and store and kill
  29:       (artifacts/pcc2widthkills/operator_reach_20260903.out);
  30:     * *** DELETE instead of decline on the write side -- the lane hazard.  `st.h
  31:       0xAABB' to GENCTL must leave GENCTL unchanged; a bare deletion stores 0xBB,
  32:       VECBASE's lane, because (uint8_t) idata is the HIGHEST-address byte ***;
  33:     * an accidental per-lane compose (GENCTL would read 0xAA);
  34:     * the value a wide READ composes -- lane order and width -- not merely survival;
  35:     * loss of the latch in either direction: dropped `|=' floods (same site twice
  36:       must announce ONCE), and one bit shared across blocks silences (two sites must
  37:       announce TWICE);
  38:     * the de-escalation clause: byte writes at the LEGAL width at every reachable
  39:       arm still land, read back through each arm's own mask, with NO decline message.
  40: 
  41:   DOES NOT CATCH, stated so an escape is outside the contract rather than an argument
  42:     * per-instance vs process-global latch state: mvme187 instantiates ONE pcc2, so no
  43:       session can hold two instances.  The bitfield is per-instance by construction;
  44:       nothing here measures it;
  45:     * pcc2c040kill -- a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends the host in
  46:       reassert_interrupts().  Every GENCTL lane value here keeps bit 0x04 CLEAR so no
  47:       row dies for that reason;
  48:     * pcc2defaultkill -- an unlisted offset (0x06, 0x14, 0x18, 0x2c ...) still ends the
  49:       host in `default:';
  50:     * pcc2counter8 -- the counter arm's raw memcpy of an 8-byte write;
  51:     * what real hardware does with the LANES of a wide write (not on disk);
  52:     * the SCCERR word-write's stored value: the arm always stores 0, so a decline and
  53:       an accept are value-identical there and the row's channel is the message.
  54: 
  55: Sentinel: PCC2W_PROBE_DONE
  56: """
  57: 
  58: import os, pty, re, select, sys, time
  59: 
  60: BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "build/gxemul"
  61: LABEL = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "local"
  62: 
  63: DUMMY = "/tmp/pcc2_dummy.bin"
  64: open(DUMMY, "wb").write(b"\x00\x00\x00\x00")
  65: 
  66: CODE = 0x00010000
  67: SCRATCH = 0x00020000
  68: PCC2 = 0xfff42000
  69: #  the five GUEST-reachable blocks (even offsets) and the seven OPERATOR-reachable ones
  70: GENCTL, T2CTL, T2ICR, SCCERR, SCCTX = 0x02, 0x16, 0x1a, 0x1c, 0x1e
  71: VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, T1CTL, T1ICR, SCCICR, SCCRX, SCSIICR = 0x03, 0x15, 0x17, 0x1b, 0x1d, 0x1f, 0x2f
  72: #  pcc2 pass 2: THE TWO FOLDED-IN ARMS HAD NO ROW AT ALL.  A seat found that the probe
  73: #  touched no offset at 0x3e or 0x3f -- twelve blocks converted, ten given rows.  MASK
  74: #  is GENCTL's byte-identical twin (store byte, reassert); GENCTL's null-repair was
  75: #  caught by `rb genctl', MASK's by nothing, a 60/60 sweep.  AND IPL IS EVEN: it is the
  76: #  SIXTH guest-reachable block, not a seventh operator one.
  77: IPL, MASK = 0x3e, 0x3f
  78: CHIPID = 0x00
  79: 
  80: 
  81: def enc(op26, rd, rs1, imm):
  82:     return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
  83: 
  84: 
  85: #  r2 = PCC2 base, r3 = scratch, r4 = load target, r5 = store source, r6 = second source
  86: #  ld.b / ld.h (0x07 / 0x06) SIGN-EXTEND: 0x82 would print as 0xffffff82.  Every row that
  87: #  asserts a VALUE uses the unsigned forms ld.bu / ld.hu (0x03 / 0x02); the survival rows
  88: #  only check exit status and keep the signed forms.
  89: def LD_B(rd, off): return enc(0x07, rd, 2, off)
  90: def LD_H(rd, off): return enc(0x06, rd, 2, off)
  91: def LD_BU(rd, off): return enc(0x03, rd, 2, off)
  92: def LD_HU(rd, off): return enc(0x02, rd, 2, off)
  93: def LD_W(rd, off): return enc(0x05, rd, 2, off)
  94: def ST_B(rs, off): return enc(0x0b, rs, 2, off)
  95: def ST_H(rs, off): return enc(0x0a, rs, 2, off)
  96: def ST_W(rs, off): return enc(0x09, rs, 2, off)
  97: I_ST_SCR = enc(0x09, 5, 3, 0)          # st  r5,r3,0   scratch word
  98: I_LD_SCR = enc(0x05, 4, 3, 0)          # ld  r4,r3,0
  99: I_BR_SELF = 0xc0000000
 100: 
 101: SENTINEL = 0xdeadbeef
 102: DEVSIG = "IGNORING write to read-only PCCTWO_CHIPID"
 103: DECL = "-- declined.  (once) ]"
 104: rows = []
 105: 
 106: 
 107: def row(name, got, want, note=""):
 108:     assert name not in [n for n, _ in rows], "DUPLICATE ROW NAME %r" % name
 109:     assert len(name) <= 15, name
 110:     ok = (got == want)
 111:     rows.append((name, ok))
 112:     print("%-16s %-12s %-12s %-46s %s" % (name, str(got), str(want), note,
 113:                                           "ok" if ok else "FAIL"))
 114:     return ok

 117: def session(cmds, settle=1.5, spin=1.2):
 118:     pid, fd = pty.fork()
 119:     if pid == 0:
 120:         os.execv(BIN, [BIN, "-V", "-x", "-E", "mvme88k", "-e", "mvme187", "-M", "64",
 121:                        "0x10000:" + DUMMY])
 122:         os._exit(127)
 123:     buf = ""
 124: 
 125:     def rd(t):
 126:         nonlocal buf
 127:         end = time.time() + t
 128:         while time.time() < end:
 129:             r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
 130:             if not r:
 131:                 continue
 132:             try:
 133:                 d = os.read(fd, 65536)
 134:                 if not d:
 135:                     return False
 136:                 buf += d.decode("latin-1", "replace")
 137:             except OSError:
 138:                 return False
 139:         return True
 140: 
 141:     alive = rd(settle)
 142:     for i, c in enumerate(cmds):
 143:         try:
 144:             os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode("latin-1"))
 145:         except OSError:
 146:             alive = False
 147:             break
 148:         if not rd(spin if i == len(cmds) - 1 else 0.25):
 149:             alive = False
 150:             break
 151:     try:
 152:         os.write(fd, b"quit\n")
 153:         rd(0.4)
 154:     except OSError:
 155:         pass
 156:     try:
 157:         os.close(fd)
 158:     except OSError:
 159:         pass
 160:     how = "nostatus"
 161:     try:
 162:         _, stat = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
 163:         if os.WIFSIGNALED(stat):
 164:             how = "sig%d" % os.WTERMSIG(stat)
 165:         elif os.WIFEXITED(stat):
 166:             how = "exit%d" % os.WEXITSTATUS(stat)
 167:     except OSError:
 168:         pass
 169:     return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive, how

 171: 
 172: def printed(t, reg):
 173:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
 174:     return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16) if m else "absent"
 175: 
 176: 
 177: def program(insns, at=CODE):
 178:     #  `at' lets a session run TWO programs: a second sequence at the SAME address finds
 179:     #  pc already there and the assignment is skipped (the Addr hazard, designed out in
 180:     #  the sgi probes with Addr.take()).  Callers needing two pass at=CODE + 0x100.
 181:     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
 182:     cmds = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, w) for i, w in enumerate(words)]
 183:     return cmds + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]

 186: def setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a", r6="0x0"):

 282: row("rb genctl lo", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x11",
 283:     "st.h of a value that FITS a byte is still declined -- the guard is on len, not idata")
 284: 
 285: # ------------------------- latch CARDINALITY (pass 2, the measuring seat's class closer)
 286: #  `latch same' and `latch two' pin ONE pair out of 66: every other session writes at most
 287: #  one BLOCK, so a bit shared by any other pair announces normally and no row moves.
 288: #  MEASURED by the builder: aliasing SCCTX onto SCCRX's bit -- a ONE-CHARACTER edit --
 289: #  left the 60-row detector at 60/60.  ONE OFFSET PER BLOCK: TxCTL serves T1CTL+T2CTL
 290: #  and TxICR serves T1ICR+T2ICR, so twelve LABELS would give only ten announces -- the
 291: #  same units slip that let "five even blocks + seven odd ones = twelve" read as all
 292: #  twelve blocks.  This row pins the CARDINALITY of the latch: any collision anywhere in
 293: #  the 12-bit space drops the count.  (Operator path for all twelve, so the odd ones are
 294: #  reachable; the guest-reachable halves have their own rows above.)
 295: BLOCKS12 = (("GENCTL", 0x02), ("VECBASE", 0x03), ("PSCALEADJ", 0x15), ("TxCTL", 0x16),
 296:             ("TxICR", 0x1a), ("SCCERR", 0x1c), ("SCCICR", 0x1d), ("SCCTX", 0x1e),
 297:             ("SCCRX", 0x1f), ("SCSIICR", 0x2f), ("IPL", 0x3e), ("MASK", 0x3f))
 298: #  VALUE 0x0003, not 0x0303: it fits a byte, so this row ALSO reds if any arm swaps its
 299: #  guard operand from len to a value test (see `rb genctl lo').  On the fix the value
 300: #  never lands, so the expectation is value-independent.
 301: t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0003" % (PCC2 + o) for _, o in BLOCKS12])
 302: row("latch all", t.count(DECL), 12,
 303:     "one wide write per BLOCK -> TWELVE announces: bit CARDINALITY, not one pair")
 304: row("latch all lv", how, "exit0", "...and the host survived all twelve blocks")
 305: 
 306: # ------------------------------------------------------------------ latch rows
 307: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0xaabb") + program([ST_H(6, GENCTL), ST_H(6, GENCTL)]))
 308: row("latch same", t.count("write to PCCTWO_GENCTL " + DECL), 1,
 309:     "same block twice -> ONE announce -- kills a dropped |= (flood)")
 310: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0x0303") + program([ST_H(6, GENCTL), ST_H(6, T2CTL)]))
 311: row("latch two", t.count(DECL), 2,
 312:     "two blocks -> TWO announces -- kills a bit shared across blocks")
 313: 
 314: # --------------------------------------------- de-escalation ACCEPT rows (byte writes)
 315: #  Removing twelve exit(1)s destroys the tripwire that announced guard growth.  So the
 316: #  legal width must still LAND at each reachable arm, through its own mask, with no
 317: #  decline message.  Expected values from the arms' own semantics.
 318: for nm, off, wr, rdv in (("genctl", GENCTL, "0x82", "0x82"),     # stores the byte; bit 0x04 clear
 319:                           ("t2ctl", T2CTL, "0x03", "0x3"),       # &= 0xfc; |= (v & 3)
 320:                           ("t2icr", T2ICR, "0x13", "0x13"),      # &= ~0x17; |= (v & 0x17)
 321:                           ("scctx", SCCTX, "0x1f", "0x1f"),      # = v & 0x1f
 322:                           ("sccerr", SCCERR, "0x5a", "0x0")):    # any write clears to 0
 323:     t, alive, how = session(setup(r5=wr) + program([ST_B(5, off), LD_BU(4, off)]) + ["print r4"])
 324:     row("acc " + nm, printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", rdv,
 325:         "st.b %s at the legal width lands through its own mask" % nm.upper())
 326:     row("acc %s nod" % nm, t.count(DECL), 0, "...with NO decline message")
 327: 
 328: # ------------------------------- OPERATOR path: the seven odd blocks via `put h'
 329: #  The m88k alignment check makes these guest-unreachable; the debugger warns on a
 330: #  misaligned put and stores anyway, straight to the handler (MEASURED pre-fix: exit1).
 331: for nm, off in (("vecbase", VECBASE), ("pscale", PSCALEADJ), ("t1ctl", T1CTL),
 332:                 ("t1icr", T1ICR), ("sccicr", SCCICR), ("sccrx", SCCRX), ("scsiicr", SCSIICR)):
 333:     t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0303" % (PCC2 + off)])
 334:     row("op " + nm, how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd %s survives" % nm.upper())
 335:     row("op %s dcl" % nm, t.count(DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
 336: 
 337: #  --------- the odd arms' FALL-THROUGH channel (pass 3, ruled a floor breach, not a disclosure)
 338: #  The block above proves each odd arm ANNOUNCES; it cannot see a guard that announces AND
 339: #  then lets the halfword land (the M_FALL shape -- decline nulled, guard kept: clause (d)).
 340: #  PRETABLE Section 2 had disclosed that as "a sentence owed".  The measuring seat asked
 341: #  whether a disclosure can discharge a floor clause; it cannot, or every breach becomes a
 342: #  sentence.  So each of the four arms gets the channel its body offers:
 343: #    SCCICR/SCCRX/SCSIICR store `idata & mask' then reassert_interrupts(): set 0x1f by
 344: #      byte (legal under both masks), decline a wide 0xaab8, read the byte back -- a landed
 345: #      write leaves 0x18 (& 0x1f) or 0x98 (& 0xdf) in an INTERRUPT CONTROL register.
 346: #    PSCALEADJ stores nothing; its write body only prints "ignored (value ...)".  A landed
 347: #      wide write therefore prints that line -- the message is the only channel, and the
 348: #      row counts it at ZERO.
 349: for nm, off in (("sccicr", SCCICR), ("sccrx", SCCRX), ("scsiicr", SCSIICR)):
 350:     t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x1f") + program([ST_B(5, off)])
 351:                             + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaab8" % (PCC2 + off)]
 352:                             + program([LD_BU(4, off)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
 353:     row("rb " + nm, printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x1f",
 354:         "%s unchanged after a declined put h -- a fall-through lands 0x18/0x98 and reasserts" % nm.upper())
 355: t, alive, how = session(["put h 0x%x, 0x0303" % (PCC2 + PSCALEADJ)])
 356: row("op pscale noig", t.count("PSCALEADJ ignored"), 0,
 357:     "a declined wide write never reaches the 'ignored' print -- fall-through would")
 358: 
 359: # --------------------------------- IPL (guest-reachable, EVEN) and MASK (operator, odd)
 360: #  IPL 0x3e: a halfword at 0x3e lands in the IPL arm.  Its write side is read-only (#119
 361: #  "ignored"), so a wide write is declined by the WIDTH latch first and a readback cannot
 362: #  tell the two refusals apart -- the channel is the message.
 363: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([I_ST_SCR, LD_H(4, IPL), I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
 364: row("hrd ipl", how, "exit0", "ld.h IPL (EVEN, the SIXTH guest-reachable block) survives")
 365: row("hrd ipl lv", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x5a5a5a5a", "...and runs on")
 366: t, alive, how = session(setup(r6="0x1234") + program([I_ST_SCR, ST_H(6, IPL), I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
 367: row("hwr ipl", how, "exit0", "st.h IPL survives")
 368: row("hwr ipl lv", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x5a5a5a5a", "...and runs on")
 369: row("hwr ipl dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_IPL " + DECL), 1,
 370:     "...declined by the WIDTH latch (not the read-only one) -- the message is the channel")
 371: #  MASK 0x3f: odd, operator path.  It is GENCTL's twin, so it gets GENCTL's readback: set
 372: #  MASK to a known byte at the legal width, decline a wide write through the debugger,
 373: #  then read the byte back through a program at a SECOND address.  A deleted guard stores
 374: #  the wrong lane (0xbb); an accidental compose stores 0xaa.  reassert_interrupts on a
 375: #  MASK byte write is harmless here: GENCTL's C040 bit is clear and nothing is pending.
 376: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12") + program([ST_B(5, MASK)])
 377:                         + ["put h 0x%x, 0xaabb" % (PCC2 + MASK)]
 378:                         + program([LD_BU(4, MASK)], at=CODE + 0x100) + ["print r4"])
 379: row("op mask", how, "exit0", "debugger put h at odd MASK survives")
 380: row("op mask dcl", t.count("write to PCCTWO_MASK " + DECL), 1, "...and was declined once")
 381: row("rb mask", printed(t, "r4") if alive else "died", "0x12",
 382:     "MASK unchanged after the declined wide write -- GENCTL's twin gets GENCTL's readback")
 383: 
 384: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------- summary
 385: print("NOROW  two device instances               -- mvme187 has ONE pcc2; per-instance latch state"
 386:       " is by construction, not measured")
 387: print("NOROW  pcc2c040kill                        -- a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends"
 388:       " the host; every GENCTL lane here keeps that bit clear")
 389: print("NOROW  pcc2defaultkill / pcc2counter8      -- other arms, other rounds")
 390: bad = [n for n, ok in rows if not ok]
 391: print("PCC2W_RESULT=%d/%d" % (len(rows) - len(bad), len(rows)))
 392: print("PCC2W_LABEL=%s" % LABEL)
 393: if bad:
 394:     print("PCC2W_RED=%s" % ",".join(bad))
 395: print("PCC2W_PROBE_DONE")

exec
