VERDICT: NO-GO

## 1. Premise audit

### (a) The 72-cell enumeration is not closed

Legend:

- `M` — isolated mutant measured red.
- `P` — measured by the pre-fix binary; each relevant row ran in a separate process.
- `A` — not measured at that block, but a specific existing channel is argued equivalent.
- `U` — unmeasured and uncovered; predicts 82/82.
- `E` — product-equivalent under the current model invariant.

| Block | Delete | `len > 2` | Exit | Alias | Fall-through | `idata > ff` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| GENCTL | M | A | P | A | M | M |
| VECBASE | A | A | P | A | **U** | A |
| PSCALEADJ | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| TxCTL | A | M | P | A | A | A |
| TxICR | M | M | P | A | A | A |
| SCCERR | A | M | P | A | E | A |
| SCCICR | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| SCCTX | M | M | P | M | A | A |
| SCCRX | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| SCSIICR | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| IPL | A | A | M | A | **U** | A |
| MASK | M | M | M | A | M | A |

Totals: 29 measured, 40 argued-equivalent channels, two uncovered, one product-equivalent = 72.

The two uncovered cells are decisive:

- VECBASE announce-then-fall-through: `op vecbase dcl` remains 1. `rb vecbase` is not a VECBASE-guard readback: it performs `ST_H(..., GENCTL)` and only observes the neighboring VECBASE lane. Removing VECBASE’s `break` changes VECBASE and prints its HUH diagnostic, but no row observes either.
- IPL announce-then-fall-through: `hwr ipl dcl` remains 1, survival and liveness remain green, and the newly reached “write to read-only … ignored” diagnostic is not counted. This is the same channel class as PSCALEADJ, but only PSCALEADJ received a negative “ignored” row.

SCCERR fall-through is equivalent in the current model: initialization makes SCCERR zero, and the only modeled write to it clears it to zero. Removing the break therefore performs a redundant zero store after the same announcement. It should be recorded as an equivalent cell, not a measured kill.

The argued cells are otherwise sound:

- Delete and `len > 2` suppress the block’s decline message, so its per-arm `dcl` row varies.
- TxCTL, TxICR, and SCCTX fall-through executes the same body/input already shown to vary their readbacks.
- Every alias between two used callers reduces the twelve unique live bit IDs to eleven.
- At any one-block `idata > 0xff` mutation, `latch all` supplies `idata=3`, suppressing exactly that block’s decline.

The shape set itself is also incomplete. The m88k producer can emit lengths 1, 2, 4, and 8 subject to alignment; the debugger supports `put h`, `put w`, and `put d` and merely warns on misalignment. Except for SCCERR, the detector supplies no four-byte write to these guards. A predicate that declines only halfwords therefore escapes.

### (b) `latch all` is a sound cardinality row

`BLOCKS12` contains twelve distinct block representatives. `d->warned` starts at zero through instance initialization, and the helper is the only operational setter.

With one caller aliased to another, the scripted events can emit only eleven distinct latch bits. Earlier benign traffic cannot restore twelve: its announcement is captured in the same PTY and it can only invoke an existing caller, not the now-orphaned enum bit. Repeated visits to either aliased block still announce once for their shared bit.

The record should call this “twelve live bits in a 32-bit field,” not “a 12-bit field.”

### (c) `op pscale noig` is real

Yes. `session()` captures the child through a PTY, including the `fatal()` output. BASE3 measured zero occurrences; `M_FALL_PSCALEADJ` measured one and failed only that row. Zero is correct on the fix because the guard announces and breaks before the ignored-write body.

### (d) The three readbacks are correct and do not die

Enumerated:

- SCCICR: `0xb8 & 0x1f = 0x18`.
- SCCRX: `0xb8 & 0xdf = 0x98`.
- SCSIICR: `0xb8 & 0xdf = 0x98`.

The pass-3c artifacts directly measured those three values, not `died`. `reassert_interrupts()` exits only through the GENCTL C040 condition; GENCTL initializes to zero and those sessions do not set it. For `0x98`, the interrupt level is zero, so the resulting index is also in range.

## 2. Smallest escaping edit

Inside the claimed 72-cell grid, remove the `break;` following VECBASE’s decline. Expected green rows include:

- `op vecbase dcl`: still 1.
- `rb vecbase`: still 0x50, because its wide write targets GENCTL rather than VECBASE.

All 82 rows should remain green.

A smaller textual escape outside the six-shape list is the two-character VECBASE predicate change:

```c
len != 1
```

to:

```c
len == 2
```

Every existing VECBASE halfword row, including `op vecbase dcl`, remains green, while `put w` reaches the body with `len == 4`. This independently disproves completeness of the chosen shape enumeration.

## 3. Option-set completeness

N/A is appropriate. This pass is validating the settled direction split, not choosing a disposition. The validation nevertheless cannot close until the floor breaches are repaired.

## 4. Record honesty

The numerical BASE and mutant results checked are consistent with their artifacts, and the frozen/live hashes for the 82-row probe and product match. The record still contains material inaccuracies:

- PRETABLE Section 2 assigns `rb vecbase` to VECBASE’s own refusal effect, but that row exercises the GENCTL guard.
- Section 5 correctly says a `dcl` row cannot detect announce-then-land, then applies that rule to only four arms and misses VECBASE and IPL.
- “Seven OPERATOR-reachable blocks” and the retained “five guest / seven operator” source comment repeat the unit error. There are six operator-only blocks, eight odd register addresses when the two T1 aliases are included, and the debugger can operationally reach all twelve block representatives.
- The product comment says “six odd ones” but its expanded list contains eight odd addresses.
- `d->warned` is a 32-bit field with twelve currently assigned bits, not a 12-bit field.
- The claimed de-escalation coverage for byte writes “at every reachable arm” exceeds the rows: PSCALEADJ and IPL lack explicit legal-byte acceptance channels, and the T1 aliases are not separately exercised at byte width.
- The inlined helper is not copied in full: its function-closing brace is missing before the first `case`.
- The disclosure says `CHANGELOG.md` is `93/0`; the reviewed worktree currently reports `98/0`. The remaining numstat entries match.
- Sections 2 and 5 are chronologically reconcilable because Section 5 is explicitly an amendment. They are not an honest final coverage account: the amendment leaves the VECBASE and IPL channel errors standing.

Required changes are an own-arm VECBASE fall-through channel, an IPL negative ignored-message channel, and per-block four-byte decline coverage—most compactly another twelve-block cardinality session using `put w`.

## 1. Premise audit

### (a) The 72-cell enumeration is not closed

Legend:

- `M` — isolated mutant measured red.
- `P` — measured by the pre-fix binary; each relevant row ran in a separate process.
- `A` — not measured at that block, but a specific existing channel is argued equivalent.
- `U` — unmeasured and uncovered; predicts 82/82.
- `E` — product-equivalent under the current model invariant.

| Block | Delete | `len > 2` | Exit | Alias | Fall-through | `idata > ff` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| GENCTL | M | A | P | A | M | M |
| VECBASE | A | A | P | A | **U** | A |
| PSCALEADJ | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| TxCTL | A | M | P | A | A | A |
| TxICR | M | M | P | A | A | A |
| SCCERR | A | M | P | A | E | A |
| SCCICR | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| SCCTX | M | M | P | M | A | A |
| SCCRX | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| SCSIICR | A | A | P | A | M | A |
| IPL | A | A | M | A | **U** | A |
| MASK | M | M | M | A | M | A |

Totals: 29 measured, 40 argued-equivalent channels, two uncovered, one product-equivalent = 72.

The two uncovered cells are decisive:

- VECBASE announce-then-fall-through: `op vecbase dcl` remains 1. `rb vecbase` is not a VECBASE-guard readback: it performs `ST_H(..., GENCTL)` and only observes the neighboring VECBASE lane. Removing VECBASE’s `break` changes VECBASE and prints its HUH diagnostic, but no row observes either.
- IPL announce-then-fall-through: `hwr ipl dcl` remains 1, survival and liveness remain green, and the newly reached “write to read-only … ignored” diagnostic is not counted. This is the same channel class as PSCALEADJ, but only PSCALEADJ received a negative “ignored” row.

SCCERR fall-through is equivalent in the current model: initialization makes SCCERR zero, and the only modeled write to it clears it to zero. Removing the break therefore performs a redundant zero store after the same announcement. It should be recorded as an equivalent cell, not a measured kill.

The argued cells are otherwise sound:

- Delete and `len > 2` suppress the block’s decline message, so its per-arm `dcl` row varies.
- TxCTL, TxICR, and SCCTX fall-through executes the same body/input already shown to vary their readbacks.
- Every alias between two used callers reduces the twelve unique live bit IDs to eleven.
- At any one-block `idata > 0xff` mutation, `latch all` supplies `idata=3`, suppressing exactly that block’s decline.

The shape set itself is also incomplete. The m88k producer can emit lengths 1, 2, 4, and 8 subject to alignment; the debugger supports `put h`, `put w`, and `put d` and merely warns on misalignment. Except for SCCERR, the detector supplies no four-byte write to these guards. A predicate that declines only halfwords therefore escapes.

### (b) `latch all` is a sound cardinality row

`BLOCKS12` contains twelve distinct block representatives. `d->warned` starts at zero through instance initialization, and the helper is the only operational setter.

With one caller aliased to another, the scripted events can emit only eleven distinct latch bits. Earlier benign traffic cannot restore twelve: its announcement is captured in the same PTY and it can only invoke an existing caller, not the now-orphaned enum bit. Repeated visits to either aliased block still announce once for their shared bit.

The record should call this “twelve live bits in a 32-bit field,” not “a 12-bit field.”

### (c) `op pscale noig` is real

Yes. `session()` captures the child through a PTY, including the `fatal()` output. BASE3 measured zero occurrences; `M_FALL_PSCALEADJ` measured one and failed only that row. Zero is correct on the fix because the guard announces and breaks before the ignored-write body.

### (d) The three readbacks are correct and do not die

Enumerated:

- SCCICR: `0xb8 & 0x1f = 0x18`.
- SCCRX: `0xb8 & 0xdf = 0x98`.
- SCSIICR: `0xb8 & 0xdf = 0x98`.

The pass-3c artifacts directly measured those three values, not `died`. `reassert_interrupts()` exits only through the GENCTL C040 condition; GENCTL initializes to zero and those sessions do not set it. For `0x98`, the interrupt level is zero, so the resulting index is also in range.

## 2. Smallest escaping edit

Inside the claimed 72-cell grid, remove the `break;` following VECBASE’s decline. Expected green rows include:

- `op vecbase dcl`: still 1.
- `rb vecbase`: still 0x50, because its wide write targets GENCTL rather than VECBASE.

All 82 rows should remain green.

A smaller textual escape outside the six-shape list is the two-character VECBASE predicate change:

```c
len != 1
```

to:

```c
len == 2
```

Every existing VECBASE halfword row, including `op vecbase dcl`, remains green, while `put w` reaches the body with `len == 4`. This independently disproves completeness of the chosen shape enumeration.

## 3. Option-set completeness

N/A is appropriate. This pass is validating the settled direction split, not choosing a disposition. The validation nevertheless cannot close until the floor breaches are repaired.

## 4. Record honesty

The numerical BASE and mutant results checked are consistent with their artifacts, and the frozen/live hashes for the 82-row probe and product match. The record still contains material inaccuracies:

- PRETABLE Section 2 assigns `rb vecbase` to VECBASE’s own refusal effect, but that row exercises the GENCTL guard.
- Section 5 correctly says a `dcl` row cannot detect announce-then-land, then applies that rule to only four arms and misses VECBASE and IPL.
- “Seven OPERATOR-reachable blocks” and the retained “five guest / seven operator” source comment repeat the unit error. There are six operator-only blocks, eight odd register addresses when the two T1 aliases are included, and the debugger can operationally reach all twelve block representatives.
- The product comment says “six odd ones” but its expanded list contains eight odd addresses.
- `d->warned` is a 32-bit field with twelve currently assigned bits, not a 12-bit field.
- The claimed de-escalation coverage for byte writes “at every reachable arm” exceeds the rows: PSCALEADJ and IPL lack explicit legal-byte acceptance channels, and the T1 aliases are not separately exercised at byte width.
- The inlined helper is not copied in full: its function-closing brace is missing before the first `case`.
- The disclosure says `CHANGELOG.md` is `93/0`; the reviewed worktree currently reports `98/0`. The remaining numstat entries match.
- Sections 2 and 5 are chronologically reconcilable because Section 5 is explicitly an amendment. They are not an honest final coverage account: the amendment leaves the VECBASE and IPL channel errors standing.

Required changes are an own-arm VECBASE fall-through channel, an IPL negative ignored-message channel, and per-block four-byte decline coverage—most compactly another twelve-block cardinality session using `put w`.
